- [ ] **Step 1: Verify the OCI Search tag-predicate syntax (spec open question 1)**

This is the one documentation lookup the plan owns. Check the structured-search query language for
a tag predicate against the official reference before writing any server-side narrowing. The CLI
probe is optional - the OCI CLI is not a dependency of this repository - so it must not stop the
step:

```bash
command -v oci >/dev/null && oci search resource structured-search --help 2>&1 | head -40 \
  || echo "OCI CLI not installed - documentation only"
```

Then read these three pages (all under `docs.oracle.com/iaas`, the Search service's own reference):

- **Running a custom free-form query** -
  `https://docs.oracle.com/en-us/iaas/Content/Search/Tasks/queryingresources_topic-To_run_a_custom_freeform_query_to_find_a_resource.htm`
  gives the defined-tag form:
  `query instance resources where (definedTags.namespace = 'rqs' && definedTags.key = 'costcenter' && definedTags.value = '1234')`
- **Tag-based search** -
  `https://docs.oracle.com/en-us/iaas/database-management/doc/tags-search-database-management.html`
  gives the freeform form: `where (freeformTags.key = '<key>' && freeformTags.value = '<value>')`
- **Search language syntax** - `https://docs.oracle.com/en-us/iaas/Content/Search/Concepts/querysyntax.htm`
  for the grammar

The operative finding is that a tag predicate is **one namespace / key / value triple per query**:
there is no way to express "any of these five tag keys" or a substring match in a single structured
search, so an `oci_view` filter with several `tag:` expressions cannot be pushed down. Record that
in the module docstring with the source URL, and **emit nothing server-side**: the client-side
`filter_model` pass runs unconditionally afterwards, so correctness never depends on it. Step 9
turns this finding into the docstring text and a test asserts the placeholder was replaced.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing tests**

Create `oci-drawio-architect/tests/test_query_tenancy_views.py`:

```python
"""Live-tenancy reader tests for the v1.5.0 metadata, provenance and filter flags."""
from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import io
import json
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

TESTS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
SCRIPTS_DIR = TESTS_DIR.parent / "scripts"
sys.path.insert(0, str(SCRIPTS_DIR))
import oci_view as ov  # noqa: E402
import query_tenancy as qt  # noqa: E402


def quiet(fn, *args, **kwargs):
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()), contextlib.redirect_stderr(io.StringIO()):
        return fn(*args, **kwargs)


VCN = "ocid1.vcn.oc1..aaaavcn"
SUBNET = "ocid1.subnet.oc1..aaaasubnet"
INSTANCE = "ocid1.instance.oc1..aaaainstance"
VNIC = "ocid1.vnic.oc1..aaaavnic"
RT = "ocid1.routetable.oc1..aaaart"
NAT = "ocid1.natgateway.oc1..aaaanat"
BUCKET = "ocid1.bucket.oc1..aaaabucket"


def bundle():
    return {"networking_topology": [{"data": {"type": "NETWORKING", "entities": [
        {"id": VCN, "type": "Vcn", "display-name": "vcn-app", "cidr-block": "10.0.0.0/16",
         "lifecycle-state": "AVAILABLE"},
        {"id": SUBNET, "type": "Subnet", "display-name": "sn-app", "cidr-block": "10.0.2.0/24",
         "vcn-id": VCN, "route-table-id": RT, "lifecycle-state": "AVAILABLE"},
        {"id": INSTANCE, "type": "Instance", "display-name": "App VM", "shape": "VM.Standard.E5.Flex",
         "availability-domain": "Uocm:PHX-AD-1", "fault-domain": "FAULT-DOMAIN-2",
         "lifecycle-state": "RUNNING"},
        {"id": VNIC, "type": "Vnic", "subnet-id": SUBNET, "private-ip": "10.0.2.47",
         "public-ip": "203.0.113.10", "hostname-label": "appvm", "lifecycle-state": "AVAILABLE"},
        {"id": RT, "type": "RouteTable", "display-name": "rt-app", "vcn-id": VCN,
         "lifecycle-state": "AVAILABLE"},
        {"id": NAT, "type": "NatGateway", "display-name": "NAT", "vcn-id": VCN,
         "lifecycle-state": "AVAILABLE"}],
        "relationships": [
            {"type": "CONTAINS", "id1": VCN, "id2": SUBNET},
            {"type": "ASSOCIATED_WITH", "id1": VNIC, "id2": INSTANCE},
            {"type": "ASSOCIATED_WITH", "id1": SUBNET, "id2": RT},
            {"type": "ROUTES_TO", "id1": RT, "id2": NAT,
             "route-rule-details": {"destination": "0.0.0.0/0"}}]}}],
        "search": [{"data": {"items": [
            {"resource-type": "Bucket", "identifier": BUCKET, "display-name": "logs-bucket",
             "lifecycle-state": "ACTIVE"}]}}]}


def item(model, address):
    for vcn in model["vcns"]:
        for sn in vcn["subnets"]:
            for it in sn["items"]:
                if it["address"] == address:
                    return it
        for coll in ("services", "controls"):
            for it in vcn[coll]:
                if it["address"] == address:
                    return it
    for it in model["services"]:
        if it["address"] == address:
            return it
    raise KeyError(address)


class VnicPropagationTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_the_vnic_ip_reaches_its_host(self):
        """9: this is where a live tenancy's private IPs actually come from."""
        model = quiet(qt.build_model, bundle(), mode="all")
        meta = item(model, INSTANCE)["metadata"]
        self.assertEqual(meta["private_ip"], "10.0.2.47")
        self.assertEqual(meta["public_ip"], "203.0.113.10")
        self.assertEqual(meta["hostname_label"], "appvm")

    def test_the_lifecycle_state_is_surfaced_into_the_metadata(self):
        model = quiet(qt.build_model, bundle(), mode="all")
        self.assertEqual(item(model, INSTANCE)["metadata"]["lifecycle_state"], "RUNNING")

    def test_a_host_without_a_vnic_simply_has_no_ip(self):
        raw = bundle()
        entities = raw["networking_topology"][0]["data"]["entities"]
        raw["networking_topology"][0]["data"]["entities"] = [e for e in entities
                                                             if e["id"] != VNIC]
        raw["networking_topology"][0]["data"]["relationships"].append(
            {"type": "CONTAINS", "id1": SUBNET, "id2": INSTANCE})
        model = quiet(qt.build_model, raw, mode="all")
        self.assertNotIn("private_ip", item(model, INSTANCE)["metadata"])


class DiscoveryTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_a_route_derived_edge_is_reachability_not_an_association(self):
        model = quiet(qt.build_model, bundle(), mode="all")
        routes = [e for e in model["edges"] if e["target"] == NAT]
        self.assertTrue(routes)
        for e in routes:
            self.assertEqual(e["discovery"], "reachability")
            # INFERRED_DISCOVERY is ("heuristic",): a route-derived edge is a
            # fact, so inferred stays False exactly as it was in 1.4.0.
            self.assertIs(e["inferred"], False)

    def test_a_discovery_selector_drops_them(self):
        model = quiet(qt.build_model, bundle(), mode="all", discovery=("association", "config"))
        self.assertEqual([e for e in model["edges"] if e["discovery"] == "reachability"], [])


class ModeTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_the_default_mode_is_participating(self):
        """A1: a tenancy dump is not a curated set."""
        self.assertEqual(qt.DEFAULT_MODE, "participating")
        model = quiet(qt.build_model, bundle())
        self.assertEqual(model["mode"], "participating")
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            item(model, BUCKET)              # a regional service with no edge

    def test_mode_all_keeps_it(self):
        self.assertTrue(item(quiet(qt.build_model, bundle(), mode="all"), BUCKET))


class FilterTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_a_client_side_filter_is_applied_to_a_saved_bundle(self):
        model = quiet(qt.build_model, bundle(), mode="all", filter_spec=["name=App VM"])
        self.assertTrue(item(model, INSTANCE))
        with self.assertRaises(KeyError):
            item(model, BUCKET)

    def test_a_tag_filter_with_no_tags_in_the_response_warns_and_matches_nothing(self):
        model = quiet(qt.build_model, bundle(), mode="all", filter_spec=["tag:Application=x"])
        self.assertEqual([v for v in model["vcns"] if v["subnets"]], [])
        self.assertTrue([w for w in model.get("warnings") or [] if "tag" in w.lower()],
                        model.get("warnings"))

    def test_tags_are_captured_when_the_response_carries_them(self):
        raw = bundle()
        for ent in raw["networking_topology"][0]["data"]["entities"]:
            if ent["id"] == INSTANCE:
                ent["freeform-tags"] = {"Application": "payments"}
        model = quiet(qt.build_model, raw, mode="all")
        self.assertEqual(item(model, INSTANCE)["tags"]["freeform"], {"Application": "payments"})


class DocstringTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_the_search_finding_is_recorded_not_left_as_a_placeholder(self):
        """Step 1's lookup must reach the module docstring with its source URL."""
        self.assertIn("docs.oracle.com", qt.__doc__)
        self.assertNotIn("<finding", qt.__doc__)


class CliTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_the_saved_bundle_path_accepts_every_new_flag(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            src = Path(tmp) / "bundle.json"
            src.write_text(json.dumps(bundle()), encoding="utf-8")
            out = Path(tmp) / "model.json"
            rc = quiet(qt.main, ["--compartment-id", "ocid1.compartment.oc1..aaaac",
                                 "--from-json", str(src), "--out", str(out),
                                 "--mode", "all", "--filter", "type=oci_core_instance",
                                 "--discovery", "association,config,reachability"])
            self.assertEqual(rc, 0)
            model = json.loads(out.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            self.assertEqual(model["mode"], "all")
            self.assertEqual(model["filter"]["include"], ["type=oci_core_instance"])
```

Replace with:

```python
# A1: a tenancy dump is not curated - it is the input the guidelines' "do not
# show every discovered OCI resource by default" is about. parse_terraform.py
# defaults to "all" instead.
DEFAULT_MODE = "participating"


def build_model(bundle: Dict[str, list], compartment_id: Optional[str] = None, vcn_id: Optional[str] = None,
                region: Optional[str] = None, inferred_edges: bool = True,
                source_path: Optional[str] = None, mode: Optional[str] = None,
                filter_spec=None, discovery=None) -> Optional[dict]:
    """Bundle of CLI responses -> MODEL (None when ``--vcn-id`` matches no VCN).

    ``mode`` defaults to ``participating`` (A1): a tenancy dump routinely holds
    dozens of regional services with no edge, and they are the main reason a
    live-tenancy diagram is unreadable.
    """
```

Then find:

```python
    model["edges"] = pt.dedupe_edges(model["edges"] + _route_edges(model, ents, rels))

```

Replace with:

```python
    model["edges"] = pt.dedupe_edges(model["edges"] + _route_edges(model, ents, rels))
    if vcn_id and not pt.select_vcn(model, vcn_id):
        return None
    mode = mode or DEFAULT_MODE
    if not inferred_edges and discovery is None:
        discovery = ov.NO_INFERRED_DISCOVERY
    parsed = ov.parse_filter(filter_spec)
    if any(e["dim"] in ("tag", "ftag", "dtag", "env", "app")
           for e in parsed["include"] + parsed["exclude"]):
        has_tags = any((e.get("tags") or {}).get("freeform") or (e.get("tags") or {}).get("defined")
                       for e in ents.values())
        if not has_tags:
            model.setdefault("warnings", []).append(
                "WARNING: a tag filter was given but the search response carried no tags; "
                "nothing can match. Re-run with --mode all to see the whole compartment.")
    model, report = ov.filter_model(model, filter_spec, mode=mode, discovery=discovery)
    model["mode"] = mode
    if report["include"] or report["exclude"]:
        model["filter"] = {"include": list(report["include"]), "exclude": list(report["exclude"])}
    model["filter_report"] = dict(report)
    return model
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Record the Step-1 finding in the module docstring**

Find, in the module docstring:

```
Usage
-----
    python3 query_tenancy.py --compartment-id OCID [--vcn-id OCID] [--profile P] [--region R]
                             [--from-json FILE] [--save-raw FILE] [--out model.json]
                             [--no-inferred-edges]
```

Replace with:

```
Filtering and mode (v1.5.0)
---------------------------
``--mode`` defaults to ``participating`` here, unlike ``parse_terraform.py``, which defaults to
``all``: a Terraform configuration is a curated set, a tenancy dump is not.  Every ``--filter`` /
``--tag`` / ``--resource-type`` / ``--subnet-id`` expression is applied CLIENT-side by
``oci_view.filter_model`` after the model is built, so correctness never depends on the search
query.  Server-side narrowing of ``oci search resource structured-search`` by tag was checked
against the Search service's query-language reference and is deliberately NOT emitted: a tag
predicate is one namespace / key / value triple per query
(``where (definedTags.namespace = 'ns' && definedTags.key = 'k' && definedTags.value = 'v')``,
``where (freeformTags.key = 'k' && freeformTags.value = 'v')``), with no disjunction over keys and
no substring form, so a multi-expression filter cannot be pushed down without changing its meaning.
Sources: https://docs.oracle.com/en-us/iaas/Content/Search/Concepts/querysyntax.htm and
https://docs.oracle.com/en-us/iaas/Content/Search/Tasks/queryingresources_topic-To_run_a_custom_freeform_query_to_find_a_resource.htm

Usage
-----
    python3 query_tenancy.py --compartment-id OCID [--vcn-id OCID] [--profile P] [--region R]
                             [--from-json FILE] [--save-raw FILE] [--out model.json]
                             [--no-inferred-edges] [--mode all|participating]
                             [--filter EXPR ...] [--tag K=V] [--resource-type TYPE]
                             [--subnet-id OCID] [--discovery K,K] [--relationships FILE]
```

**Files:**

- Modify: `oci-drawio-architect/commands/drawio-architect.md` (Steps 0, 1, 2, 3, 5.4, 6, the settings reference, the failure table)

**Interfaces:**

- Produces: `ANSWERED_KEYS = ("purpose",)`; `FIELD_ORDER` gains `"purpose"` in a stable position; the command's Step 1 purpose question and Step 0 `purpose:` key.
- Consumed by: Task 15 (the SKILL and README describe the same question).

**Documentation runs slightly ahead of the code here, on purpose.** The command text this task
writes names `--filter`, `--tag`, `--resource-type`, `--compartment`, `--subnet` and
`--mode participating` on `parse_terraform.py` (Task 11, the same group) and `--subnet-id` on
`query_tenancy.py` (Task 12, the next group). Nothing in this task executes those flags - the tests
assert on the markdown - so the group stays green, and by the end of the release every flag named
exists. `test_every_new_layout_flag_is_named_in_the_command` only checks the layout CLI's flags,
which all exist by the end of group 2.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `oci-drawio-architect/tests/test_detect_purpose.py`:

```python
"""Settings tests for the one persisted view choice (A4) and the command's Step 1 question."""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
import sys
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

TESTS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
PLUGIN_ROOT = TESTS_DIR.parent
SCRIPTS_DIR = PLUGIN_ROOT / "scripts"
sys.path.insert(0, str(SCRIPTS_DIR))
import detect_settings as ds  # noqa: E402
import oci_view as ov  # noqa: E402

COMMAND = (PLUGIN_ROOT / "commands" / "drawio-architect.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")


class SettingsTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_purpose_has_a_stable_place_in_the_frontmatter(self):
        self.assertIn("purpose", ds.FIELD_ORDER)
        text = ds.to_yaml_frontmatter({"region": "us-ashburn-1", "purpose": "security",
                                       "terraform_dir": "infra"})
        lines = text.splitlines()
        self.assertLess(lines.index('purpose: "security"'), lines.index('terraform_dir: "infra"'))
        self.assertGreater(lines.index('purpose: "security"'), lines.index('region: "us-ashburn-1"'))

    def test_purpose_is_answered_never_detected(self):
        """A4 / Step 0: a view choice is never detected, only answered and remembered."""
        self.assertEqual(ds.ANSWERED_KEYS, ("purpose",))
        for key in ds.ANSWERED_KEYS:
            self.assertNotIn(key, ds.USEFUL_KEYS)
        detected = ds.detect(str(TESTS_DIR / "fixtures" / "detect"), query_cli=False)
        self.assertNotIn("purpose", detected)

    def test_a_purpose_alone_is_not_a_useful_settings_file(self):
        self.assertFalse(ds.has_useful_settings({"purpose": "network"}))

    def test_no_other_view_key_is_persisted(self):
        for key in ("detail", "label_mode", "layers", "filter", "mode", "global_services"):
            self.assertNotIn(key, ds.FIELD_ORDER, key)


class CommandTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_step_1_asks_the_purpose_with_the_six_verbatim_names(self):
        for title in ov.PURPOSE_TITLES.values():
            self.assertIn(title, COMMAND, title)
        self.assertIn("AskUserQuestion", COMMAND)

    def test_step_0_documents_the_persisted_purpose_key(self):
        self.assertIn("purpose:", COMMAND)
        self.assertRegex(COMMAND, r"purpose[^\n]*remember")

    def test_step_2_states_the_per_front_end_mode_default(self):
        self.assertRegex(COMMAND, r"--mode[^\n]*participating")
        self.assertIn("query_tenancy.py", COMMAND)

    def test_step_2_says_a_live_tenancy_is_cut_down_before_the_model_is_written(self):
        self.assertRegex(COMMAND, r"before the model is written")

    def test_step_5_checks_the_captions_and_the_layers_panel(self):
        self.assertRegex(COMMAND, r"no OCID")
        self.assertRegex(COMMAND, r"layers panel")

    def test_step_6_reports_the_view_and_how_to_toggle_a_layer(self):
        self.assertRegex(COMMAND, r"Cmd\+Shift\+L|Ctrl\+Shift\+L")

    def test_every_new_layout_flag_is_named_in_the_command(self):
        for flag in ("--purpose", "--detail", "--label-mode", "--label-fields", "--layers",
                     "--hidden-layers", "--filter", "--mode", "--discovery",
                     "--global-services"):
            self.assertIn(flag, COMMAND, flag)

    def test_the_command_names_no_forbidden_identity(self):
        self.assertNotRegex(COMMAND, re.compile(r"co-authored|generated with", re.I))
```

```markdown
6. View choices (not settings, never detected): the canvas is `locations: "outside"` and the subnet labels are two lines unless the user asks otherwise. Replace with:
```

```markdown
6. View choices are never *detected*, only answered. The canvas is `locations: "outside"` and the subnet labels are two lines unless the user asks otherwise. Offer `show_compartments` only when the tenancy has more than one compartment in play and the user asks "show the compartments" - a view with every compartment drawn is unreadable, which is why it is off by default. The v1.4.0 choices are flags on the layout CLI: `--locations`, `--gateway-edge`, `--subnet-label`, `--attachment-style`, `--show-compartments`.
7. **`purpose` is the one view choice that is remembered.** If the settings file has `purpose:`, use it and say so; otherwise Step 1 asks for it and you write it back into the settings file. Nothing else about the view is persisted: `detail`, `label_mode`, `label_fields`, `layers`, `filter`, `mode` and `global_services` are per-run choices, and a remembered filter that has gone stale silently produces a wrong diagram.
```

Then find:

```markdown
## Step 1 - Input

Classify `$ARGUMENTS`; if empty, ask one AskUserQuestion with these five options.
```

Replace with:

```markdown
## Step 1 - Input and purpose

1. Classify `$ARGUMENTS`; if empty, ask one AskUserQuestion with these five options.
2. **Ask the diagram's purpose** with a second `AskUserQuestion` - unless `purpose:` is already in the settings file, in which case state which one is in force and move on. The six options, verbatim:

| Option | `--purpose` | Pick it when |
|--------|-------------|--------------|
| Network topology | `network` | The reader asks "how is this wired" - subnets, gateways, route tables, security lists. Today's default look |
| Application / data flow | `dataflow` | The reader follows a request through the system; only the participating resources, ports on every connector |
| Security architecture | `security` | The reader audits controls - NSGs and security lists in front, IAM and policies in their own tenancy bucket |
| Resource / inventory view | `inventory` | The reader wants what exists and where, not how it talks - no connectors, compartments drawn |
| Dependency / relationship view | `dependency` | The reader asks what depends on what, and wants to know how each relationship was discovered |
| Deployment / high-availability architecture | `ha` | The reader checks resiliency - availability and fault domains in every caption |

3. Write the answer back into `.claude/oci-drawio-architect.local.md` as `purpose: "<value>"`.
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Document the filter, mode and discovery flags in Step 2**

Find:

```markdown
1. Terraform present: `python3 "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/parse_terraform.py" [TF_DIR] [--plan-json FILE | --state-json FILE] [--vcn NAME] --out model.json`, then read `model.json`. Add `--no-inferred-edges` when you want only edges backed by explicit Terraform references.
2. Live tenancy (EXPERIMENTAL, needs the OCI CLI): `python3 "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/query_tenancy.py" --compartment-id OCID [--vcn-id OCID] [--profile P] [--region R] [--from-json FILE] --out model.json`. Tell the user the mode is experimental and to verify resource counts.
```

Replace with:

```markdown
1. Terraform present: `python3 "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/parse_terraform.py" [TF_DIR] [--plan-json FILE | --state-json FILE] [--vcn NAME] --out model.json`, then read `model.json`. Add `--no-inferred-edges` when you want only edges backed by explicit Terraform references. Scope it with `--filter EXPR` (repeatable), or its sugar `--tag K=V`, `--compartment NAME`, `--subnet NAME`, `--resource-type TYPE`. **`--mode` defaults to `all` here**: a Terraform configuration is already a curated set. Pass `--mode participating` to keep only what takes part in the architecture. A plan or state JSON also carries the private IPs, the lifecycle states and the tags that the label modes and the filters need - prefer it over bare HCL when it exists.
2. Live tenancy (EXPERIMENTAL, needs the OCI CLI): `python3 "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/query_tenancy.py" --compartment-id OCID [--vcn-id OCID] [--profile P] [--region R] [--from-json FILE] --out model.json`. Tell the user the mode is experimental and to verify resource counts. **`--mode` defaults to `participating` here**, because a tenancy dump is not curated; `--mode all` shows everything. Cut a live tenancy down **before the model is written** - with `--filter` / `--tag` / `--resource-type` / `--subnet-id` on this command, not by editing `model.json` afterwards - so the counts you report are the counts you drew.
```

Find:

```markdown
   13. Grouping boxes are opt-in: add a `subnet.groups[]` entry of type `oke_cluster` around an OKE cluster and its node pools (the parser does this for you when they share a subnet), and `tier` / `user_group` boxes only when the user asks for them. Members must live in the container that owns the box.
```

Replace with:

```markdown
   13. Grouping boxes are opt-in: add a `subnet.groups[]` entry of type `oke_cluster` around an OKE cluster and its node pools (the parser does this for you when they share a subnet), and `tier` / `user_group` boxes only when the user asks for them. Members must live in the container that owns the box.
   14. **Captions are rendered, not hand-written, since 1.5.0.** Put the display name in `label` and the rest in `metadata`: `private_ip`, `public_ip`, `fqdn`, `ports` (`"TCP/22, 8088"`), `compartment`, `availability_domain`, `fault_domain`, `lifecycle_state`, `shape`. The default `label_mode: "network"` renders name + private IP + ports; `minimal` renders the name alone and `detailed` adds AD / FD and the compartment. **An OCID is never rendered in a caption in any mode** - it belongs in `metadata` and the tooltip. A value that is already in the authored `label` is never repeated, so a hand-written caption keeps rendering exactly as written.
   15. Tags go in `item["tags"] = {"freeform": {...}, "defined": {"<ns>.<key>": ...}}`, never in `metadata` (which is scalars only). They are what `--filter tag:K=V`, `env=` and `app=` match on.
   16. The view keys go at the top level of the model: `purpose`, `detail`, `label_mode`, `label_fields`, `label_tag_keys`, `layers`, `hidden_layers`, `filter`, `mode`, `global_services`, `show_edges`. The equivalent layout-CLI flags are `--purpose`, `--detail`, `--label-mode`, `--label-fields`, `--label-tag-keys`, `--layers`, `--hidden-layers`, `--filter`, `--mode`, `--discovery`, `--annotate-discovery`, `--global-services`, `--no-edges`. An explicit key always beats the purpose.
```

```markdown
| checker exit 2 | File missing, not XML, or the checker was moved away from `drawio_builder.py` |
```

```markdown
| `filter: '...' is not '[!]<dimension>[:<key>]<op><value>...'` | Use `=` (exact) or `~` (substring), e.g. `tag:Application=payments`, `!type=oci_core_nat_gateway` |
| A `tag:` filter matches nothing on a live tenancy | The search response carried no tags; the model has none. Re-run with `--mode all` and filter on `name` / `type` instead |
| The diagram is emptier than expected | `--mode participating` pruned it, or a `--filter` did; `layout_info["pruned"]` and `layout_info["filter"]` say how much. `--mode all` and an empty `--filter` restore everything |
| The layers panel is empty | `layers` defaults to `off`; pass `--layers auto` |
| Captions lost the shape line | 1.5.0 moved it to `metadata.shape`; `--label-mode minimal --label-fields display_name,shape` renders the exact 1.4 caption back |
```

### Task 14: Examples - the reference sample's metadata migration, two demo pages and the screenshots (spec 10, 11, A3)

**Files:**
- Modify: `oci-drawio-architect/examples/generate_reference_layout.py` (`MODEL`, docstring)
- Modify: `oci-drawio-architect/examples/generate_demo_diagram.py` (docstring, `build`)
- Modify: `OCI_Architecture.drawio` (regenerated)
- Modify: `screenshots/*.png`, `Screens/*.png` (regenerated only if they changed)
- Create: `oci-drawio-architect/tests/test_examples_views.py`

**Touches:** `oci-drawio-architect/examples/generate_reference_layout.py`, `oci-drawio-architect/examples/generate_demo_diagram.py`, `OCI_Architecture.drawio`, `screenshots/`, `Screens/`, `oci-drawio-architect/tests/test_examples_views.py`

**Depends on:** Task 10

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the whole resolved view, `write_diagram(..., purpose=, detail=, label_mode=, layers=)`, `build_diagram`, `append_pages`.
- Produces: the reference `MODEL` with `metadata.private_ip` / `metadata.ports` / `metadata.shape` instead of authored values, and a six-page demo.
- Consumed by: Task 15 (the READMEs describe six pages), Task 16 (the release gate renders them).

**Two things to know before starting.** The sample's single existing metadata entry is
`{"ip": "10.0.0.23"}`, and `oci_view._field_texts("private_ip")` reads `private_ip` then
`ip_address` - never `ip` - so the migration is a **rename**, not an addition; leaving the old key
would render nothing. And A3's "rendered text is identical" does **not** hold after the migration:
the load balancer gains an `HTTPS/443` line and the ADB gains a `1522` line, because the default
`network` mode renders three fields where the authored captions carried one or two. That is
accepted by design, is what this task's tests expect, is recorded as a deviation in the Self-review
and is named in the CHANGELOG.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `oci-drawio-architect/tests/test_examples_views.py`:

```python
"""Example tests for the v1.5.0 reference-sample migration (A3) and the six-page demo."""
from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import io
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

TESTS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
PLUGIN_ROOT = TESTS_DIR.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(PLUGIN_ROOT / "scripts"))
import check_overlaps  # noqa: E402
import oci_layout as ol  # noqa: E402


def quiet(fn, *args, **kwargs):
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()), contextlib.redirect_stderr(io.StringIO()):
        return fn(*args, **kwargs)


def caption_of(d, cid):
    """The rendered caption, back in plain text (the registry stores it escaped)."""
    return (d._cells[d._cells[cid]["label_id"]]["label"]
            .replace("<br>", "\n").replace("&amp;", "&"))


class ExamplesPathMixin:
    """``examples/`` on sys.path for the duration of the class, exactly as ExamplesTests does.

    Inserting it permanently at import time would leak into every other test
    module in the same process.
    """

    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        sys.path.insert(0, str(PLUGIN_ROOT / "examples"))
        cls.addClassCleanup(sys.path.remove, str(PLUGIN_ROOT / "examples"))


class ReferenceMigrationTests(ExamplesPathMixin, unittest.TestCase):
    def model(self):
        from generate_reference_layout import MODEL
        return MODEL

    def items(self):
        out = {}
        for vcn in self.model()["vcns"]:
            for sn in vcn["subnets"]:
                for it in sn["items"]:
                    out[it["address"]] = it
            for it in vcn["services"]:
                out[it["address"]] = it
        return out

    def test_the_load_balancer_renders_its_ip_from_the_metadata(self):
        """A3 / V5: the sample exercises the renderer instead of bypassing it.

        The network mode renders three fields, so this caption gains an
        HTTPS/443 line that 1.4.0 did not draw. That is the one accepted
        deviation from A3's "rendered text identical" (see the Self-review).
        """
        lb = self.items()["lb"]
        self.assertEqual(lb["label"], "Load Balancer")
        self.assertEqual(lb["metadata"]["private_ip"], "10.0.0.23")
        d = quiet(ol.build_diagram, self.model())
        self.assertEqual(caption_of(d, "lb"), "Load Balancer\n10.0.0.23\nHTTPS/443")

    def test_an_item_whose_ip_is_not_the_last_authored_line_keeps_its_caption(self):
        """V5's dedupe rule: the metadata is provenance, the caption is unchanged."""
        vm = self.items()["app-vm"]
        self.assertEqual(vm["label"], "App VM\n10.0.1.251\n16 OCPU / 96 GB")
        self.assertEqual(vm["metadata"]["private_ip"], "10.0.1.251")
        d = quiet(ol.build_diagram, self.model())
        self.assertEqual(caption_of(d, "app-vm"), "App VM\n10.0.1.251\n16 OCPU / 96 GB")

    def test_the_database_and_the_load_balancer_carry_ports(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.items()["adb"]["metadata"]["ports"], "1522")
        self.assertEqual(self.items()["lb"]["metadata"]["ports"], "HTTPS/443")

    def test_every_metadata_address_and_port_reaches_the_rendered_caption(self):
        for address, it in self.items().items():
            caption = ol.ov.render_caption(it, ol._DEFAULT_VIEW, type_labels=ol._type_labels())
            for field in ("private_ip", "ports"):
                value = (it.get("metadata") or {}).get(field)
                if value:
                    self.assertIn(value, caption, f"{address}.{field}")

    def test_the_sample_still_uses_the_network_label_mode(self):
        self.assertNotIn("label_mode", self.model())
        self.assertEqual(quiet(ol.build_diagram, self.model()).layout_info["view"]["label_mode"],
                         "network")

    def test_the_regenerated_sample_still_passes_the_strict_gate(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            out = quiet(ol.write_diagram, self.model(), Path(tmp) / "ref.drawio")
            self.assertEqual(quiet(check_overlaps.main, ["--strict", str(out)]), 0)


class DemoPageTests(ExamplesPathMixin, unittest.TestCase):
    def test_the_demo_has_six_pages_with_the_expected_names(self):
        import generate_demo_diagram as demo
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            out = Path(tmp) / "demo.drawio"
            quiet(demo.build, out)
            text = out.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        # append_pages appends and add_page("Security") runs last, so the order
        # is fixed: 1 Architecture, 2 DRG as a box, 3 Compartments and OKE,
        # 4 Executive overview, 5 Engineering detail, 6 Security.
        names = [n for n in ("Architecture", "DRG as a box", "Compartments and OKE",
                             "Executive overview", "Engineering detail", "Security")]
        self.assertEqual([text.index(f'name="{n}"') for n in names],
                         sorted(text.index(f'name="{n}"') for n in names))
        self.assertEqual(text.count("<diagram "), 6)

    def test_page_4_is_the_executive_view_with_minimal_captions(self):
        import generate_demo_diagram as demo
        d = quiet(ol.build_diagram, demo.DEMO_MODEL, page_name="Executive overview",
                  detail="executive", label_mode="minimal")
        self.assertEqual([cid for cid, e in d._cells.items() if e.get("badge")], [])
        self.assertEqual(d.layout_info["view"]["label_mode"], "minimal")

    def test_page_5_is_the_engineering_view_with_layers_and_routes_hidden(self):
        import generate_demo_diagram as demo
        d = quiet(ol.build_diagram, demo.DEMO_MODEL, page_name="Engineering detail",
                  detail="engineering", label_mode="detailed", layers="auto",
                  hidden_layers=["routes"])
        self.assertIn("routes", d.layout_info["layers"]["enabled"])
        self.assertEqual(d.layout_info["layers"]["hidden"], ["routes"])
        self.assertEqual(d.layout_info["view"]["label_mode"], "detailed")

    def test_the_whole_demo_passes_the_strict_gate(self):
        import generate_demo_diagram as demo
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            out = Path(tmp) / "demo.drawio"
            quiet(demo.build, out)
            self.assertEqual(quiet(check_overlaps.main, ["--strict", str(out)]), 0)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the CHANGELOG entry**

In `oci-drawio-architect/CHANGELOG.md`, find:

```markdown
## [1.4.0] - 2026-09-18
```

Insert directly above it:

```markdown
## [1.5.0] - 2026-09-19

View and detail controls: one discovered topology, several readable views. Captions become a rendered field list, the diagram can be emitted onto real draw.io layers, four detail levels and six purpose presets compose the whole view, one filter predicate is shared by the parser, the live-tenancy reader and the layout CLI, and IAM / Policies / Audit / Public DNS can go into a tenancy-scoped bucket. Schema stays 2 - every new key is optional and a 1.4.x model renders with identical geometry; only the caption text of a **parsed** model changes, and one key brings the old caption back. Design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-19-v1.5.0-view-and-detail-controls-design.md`.

### Added
- **Label modes** (`label_mode`, `label_fields`, `label_tag_keys`): `minimal`, `network` (the new default) and `detailed` over a documented field vocabulary - display name, resource type, private / public IP, CIDR, FQDN, port / protocol, compartment, AD / FD, lifecycle, tags. An **OCID is never rendered in a caption in any mode**. An authored caption is kept verbatim and a field whose value it already contains is skipped, so a hand-written model renders exactly as before. `LABEL_H_DETAILED = 88` and `LABEL_LINE_BUDGET` grow the caption box, never the slot or the column pitch. The exact 1.4.0 caption of a parsed model comes back with `--label-mode minimal --label-fields display_name,shape`.
- **View layers** (`layers`, `hidden_layers`, off by default): `routes`, `security`, `iam`, `dataflow`, `management` and `associations` become real draw.io layers over a base layer named `Network`, toggled with `Cmd/Ctrl+Shift+L`. The layer pass runs last and only re-parents cells, so enabling layers cannot move a pixel; a layer with no content is not created; a PNG export renders only the visible layers.
- **Detail levels** (`detail`): `executive`, `application`, `network` (the default, today's output - including the DRG route-table badge strip) and `engineering`, each a row of content gates plus defaults for the label mode and the layer set. A gate that removes content **hides its layer** when layers are on and drops the cells when they are off, for the two badge gates whose content is layer-borne; the DRG route-table strip consumes layout space, so it is never layer-gated.
- **Filtering** (`filter`, `--filter`, `--tag`, `--compartment`, `--subnet`, `--resource-type`): one predicate over tags, compartment, region, VCN, subnet, resource type, icon, name, address, environment and application, applied identically by `parse_terraform.py`, `query_tenancy.py` and `oci_layout.py`. Expressions AND across dimensions and OR within one; `!` excludes; `~` is a substring match; an empty include means everything. Structure survives unless an expression names it.
- **Participating mode** (`mode`): `all` (the default for Terraform) or `participating` (the default for a live tenancy) - the second keeps only what takes part in the architecture.
- **Relationship provenance** (`edges[].discovery`): `association`, `config`, `reachability`, `tag`, `observed`, `user` and the plugin's own `heuristic`. `inferred` is still written and derived from it. `--discovery` selects; `--annotate-discovery` puts the provenance in the connector's tooltip; it never changes a connector style. `--relationships FILE` merges a JSON sidecar as `user` edges. `--no-inferred-edges` is a documented alias.
- **Global-services bucket** (`global_services: "bucket"`, off by default): IAM, Policies, Audit and public DNS in a tenancy container below the region, with the compartment list when the compartment containers are not drawn.
- **Purpose presets** (`purpose`, `--purpose`): `network`, `dataflow`, `security`, `inventory`, `dependency`, `ha` - the six purposes the team's diagram guidelines name, each a composition of everything above, asked once in the command's Step 1 and remembered in `.claude/oci-drawio-architect.local.md`.
- **Tags and caption metadata in the parser**: `freeform_tags` and `defined_tags` are kept; `metadata` gains `private_ip`, `public_ip`, `fqdn`, `hostname_label`, `lifecycle_state`, `compartment` and `ports`. The live-tenancy reader propagates the VNIC's addresses to its host.
- New module `scripts/oci_view.py` (standard library only), new builder primitives `set_base_layer_name`, `layer_ids`, `icon_footprint_h`, `DrawioBuilder(max_label_lines=)`, `style_extra=` on `add_icon` / `add_badge`, `tooltip=` / `metadata=` on `add_edge`, and the legend row kinds `("layer", ...)` and `("note", ...)`.
- A `tagged_app` Terraform fixture (two applications, two environments, freeform and defined tags, an unresolvable tag value, a state JSON with a computed private IP).

### Changed
- **One default flip**: `label_mode` defaults to `network`, so a **parsed** model's captions change - the compute / database shape line moves out of the caption into `metadata.shape`, and a private IP and a port list appear when the input carries them. A hand-written model is unaffected. `--label-mode minimal --label-fields display_name,shape` renders the exact 1.4 caption back.
- `subnet_label` accepts a third value `name` (no CIDR), which the executive and application levels select automatically.
- `is_regional()` is now expressed over the three-valued `oci_topology.service_scope()`; it answers exactly as before for every 1.4.x input.
- The reference sample keeps its addresses and ports in `metadata` instead of baked into the captions. Two of its captions therefore gain a line that the `network` mode renders and the authored strings did not carry: the load balancer gains `HTTPS/443` and the Autonomous Database gains `1522`. Nothing else in the sample moves. The demo grows an "Executive overview" (page 4) and an "Engineering detail" (page 5) page, so `Security` becomes page 6.
- `validate_model()` accepts every new key and rejects an unknown `label_fields` entry, layer name, mode, purpose, detail level or `discovery` value; `summarise()` reports the filter and prune counts.

### Fixed
- Validator rule 7 no longer reports a badge moved onto a view layer as foreign to the subnet or icon it decorates: a leaf whose `ociHost` names the container, or a cell inside it, is exempt.
- `DrawioBuilder.validate()` finally passes `max_label_lines` to `validate_registry`, which has accepted the parameter since 1.2.0 and was never given it.
- `query_tenancy._route_edges` labelled route-derived edges as explicit associations; they are `discovery="reachability"` now.

```

- [ ] **Step 6: Add four `gotchas.md` entries**

Replace `(v1.4.0)` with `(v1.5.0)` in the title and the "error texts are quoted verbatim" sentence,
then append four numbered sections after `## 23`. Deviation from spec 10, recorded deliberately:
the spec asked for the nested-cell gotcha to lead the file; the file is ordered oldest-first and
renumbering twenty-three entries would invalidate every cross-reference, so it is appended as
`## 24` and the "hiding the base layer blanks the page" warning is folded into it rather than
given an entry of its own.

```markdown
## 24. A nested cell cannot be on its own layer

In draw.io a layer is a child of the root and a cell belongs to the layer of its **top-level
ancestor**, so hiding a layer hides whole subtrees. A workload icon inside a subnet cannot be
toggled independently of its subnet, and emitting the IP as a separate text cell does not help -
that cell would be a child of the same subnet. This is why only page-level annotation is layered
(the badges, which are re-parented after layout, and the connectors) and why "Resource IPs" and
"Ports and protocols" are caption FIELDS rather than layers; `--layers ips` is accepted and
rewritten to `private_ip,public_ip` with a note on stderr. Hiding the base `Network` layer blanks
the page.

## 25. *migration* - v1.5.0 changes parsed captions; one key brings the old ones back

`label_mode` defaults to `network`: name, private IP, port / protocol. For a **hand-written** model
nothing changes - the authored caption is kept verbatim and a field whose value it already contains
is skipped. For a **parser- or tenancy-produced** model two things change: the compute / database
shape line moves out of the caption into `metadata.shape`, and a private IP and a port list appear
when the input carries them (a plan / state JSON, or a live tenancy; bare HCL has neither).
`--label-mode minimal` gives the bare display name; `--label-mode minimal --label-fields
display_name,shape` renders the exact 1.4 caption back, because `shape` is a caption field of its
own. Everything else added in 1.5.0 is off by default: `layers: "off"`, `detail: "network"`, an empty `filter`, `mode: "all"`,
`global_services: "osn"`, `show_edges: true`, no `purpose`.

## 26. An empty `include` means everything

`filter: {"include": [], "exclude": []}` is the identity, not "nothing". Expressions AND across
dimensions and OR within one, so `--filter tag:Application=payments --filter tag:Environment=prod`
means both, while `--filter vcn=a --filter vcn=b` means either. `exclude` always wins. Structure -
gateways, DRGs, attachments, subnets, VCNs - is not predicated unless a `vcn=`, `subnet=`, `type=`
or `name=` expression names it directly; a subnet the filter empties is dropped, and so is a VCN
with nothing left, unless `keep_empty` is set. An edge whose endpoint disappeared is dropped and
counted: no stub node is ever drawn.

## 27. `--mode participating` is the default for a live tenancy, not for Terraform

A Terraform configuration is already a curated set, so `parse_terraform.py` defaults to
`--mode all`. A tenancy dump is not, so `query_tenancy.py` defaults to `--mode participating` and
routinely drops dozens of regional services that have no edge. `layout_info["pruned"]` always says
how many. If a diagram looks emptier than the tenancy, that is the first thing to check.
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Check the release-gate greps pass on the docs**

Run (the pattern never appears in the repository; set it in the shell first). This is the v1.4.0
release-gate form: it scans the shippable tree only - `oci-drawio-architect`, the root `README.md`
and `CLAUDE.md`, never `docs/superpowers`, `dev.to/` or an untracked `.claude/*.local.md` - it
prints no paths (`-h`) so the pattern cannot leak into a transcript, and it keeps the three
attribution terms the bare name grep would miss:

```bash
export FORBIDDEN='<pipe-separated names that must never appear>'
grep -rhniE "${FORBIDDEN:?export FORBIDDEN as the forbidden-names regex}|anthropic|generated with|co-authored|noreply@" \
  --include="*.py" --include="*.md" --include="*.json" --include="*.sh" \
  oci-drawio-architect README.md CLAUDE.md | grep -viE "claude\.ai/code"
git log --format=%B origin/main..HEAD | grep -inE "co-authored|generated with|assistant|anthropic" \
  && echo "FOUND" || echo "clean"
```

Expected: no output from the first command (the `claude.ai/code` link in the plugin README is the

**Depends on:** Task 15

**Interfaces:**

- Produces: the evidence that 1.5.0 is releasable.

### 2. Decisions taken beyond the specification

1. **An explicitly-authored `detail` beats the purpose; a purpose-derived one does not** (2.7). Spec 6.1 gives one order, "detail preset -> purpose preset", but 6.9 gives `security` a `label_fields` while its detail level `network` gives a `label_mode` - under a single order the purpose's own field list would be dead. `pick()` therefore consults the detail preset before the purpose only when `detail` was set explicitly, and after it otherwise. `--detail engineering --purpose security` gives engineering's gates; `--purpose security` alone gives security's fields.
2. **A purpose sets the layer SET and the hidden set, never the layer MODE** (2.3, 10.1). Spec 6.9's table names visible and hidden layers per purpose, but V3/A5 keep layers opt-in and 6.2's own example passes `--layers auto --purpose dataflow` together. So `--purpose dataflow` alone leaves `layers: "off"` and the purpose's "hidden" layers become content drops (V4); `--layers auto` then uses the purpose's set.
3. **A caption renders one field per LINE**, not the guidelines' inline `A / B / C` (2.11). The guidelines write the modes inline as a compact notation; the caption box is `LABEL_W = 105` px wide, so one field per line is the same information in the geometry the recipe already has, and it is what every existing authored caption already does.
4. **`render_caption` takes a `type_labels` mapping** (2.11, 5.4). Spec 6.3 sources `resource_type` from `RESOURCE_ICONS[type][1]`, which lives in `parse_terraform`; `oci_view` may not import it (V9). The recipe - which already depends on both - joins them in `_type_labels()`, lazily and cached, with `humanise_type()` as the fallback for a model built outside the parser.
5. **`add_edge` gains `tooltip=` / `metadata=`** (1.22b). Spec 6.8 asks for `--annotate-discovery` to put the provenance "in the edge tooltip", and no edge could carry one: `add_edge` emitted a bare `mxCell`. The `<object>` wrapper is the same mechanism `_emit_vertex` already uses and `_iter_cells` already unwraps, and the router keeps a reference to the inner `mxCell`, so the routed geometry is untouched (proved by 1.22a's fourth test).
6. **The `gateways` and `osn` gates are table rows with no code path** (7.1's `test_the_gate_table_keeps_gateways_and_the_osn_on_at_every_level`). Every one of the four levels sets both True, so a code path would be untestable dead code; the rows exist so a 1.6.0 level can drop them, and a test pins that no 1.5.0 level does.
7. **`is_regional` is re-expressed over `service_scope`, not replaced** (3.4). With `global_services: "osn"` an IAM item must still be regional, so `is_regional(item) == service_scope(item) in ("regional", "global")` and every v1.4.0 topology test keeps passing unchanged - which is what 3.11 verifies.
8. **Tags are inherited item -> subnet -> VCN by the filter** (4.7). A VCN tagged `Application=payments` scopes everything drawn inside it, which is what a reader asking for "the payments application" means; without inheritance a `tag:` filter on a stack that tags only at the VCN level would match nothing.
9. **`oci_view.model_addresses` duplicates `parse_terraform.model_addresses`** (4.7), pinned by `AddressWalkTests` (11.2) across all four fixtures. The alternative - importing the parser into `oci_view` - inverts the dependency V9 exists to protect. The same pattern the repo already uses for `ATTACHMENT_STYLE_MODES` and `HUB_KINDS`.
10. **`INFERRED_DISCOVERY` is `("heuristic",)` and `--no-inferred-edges` keeps `reachability`** (2.3). Spec 6.8 derives `inferred` from `discovery in ("reachability", "tag", "observed")`, which would flip `inferred` to True for the route-derived edges `query_tenancy` already emits and break four existing assertions. `inferred` meant "a plausibility guess" in 1.4.0, and in 1.5.0 that is exactly `heuristic`, so the boolean keeps every one of its 1.4.0 answers and the `--no-inferred-edges` alias `association,config,reachability,user` drops precisely the `inferred=True` edges. The two constants are defined separately with that comment.
11. **The executive level draws a DRG connector straight to the VCN** (7.5). Spec 6.4 says "no attachment boxes (the DRG connects straight to the VCN border)" without saying who emits the connector; dropping the boxes silently would leave the DRG unconnected. `_layout_drg_column` emits the pending entry with the DRG glyph as source, and registers each attachment's address to the glyph so a model edge that names it keeps resolving.
12. **`filter_report` is written into the model and popped before it is serialised** (11.11, 12.7). `summarise()` needs the counts and `validate_model()` must not see a key the schema does not define; writing then popping keeps both true without a second return value through three call sites.
13. **The demo's `Security` page stays last, and becomes page 6** (14.5). `append_pages` appends and `add_page("Security")` runs after it, so the emitted order is 1 Architecture, 2 DRG as a box, 3 Compartments and OKE, 4 Executive overview, 5 Engineering detail, 6 Security. The alternative - inserting the new pages before Security to keep it at 4 - would renumber nothing useful and would still move the two other pages; the docstring, the test's order assertion and Task 16's `-p 4` / `-p 5` renders all follow the emitted order.
14. **Two helper additions that the spec does not name**: `_icon_kwargs(view)` (5.4), which passes `label_h` only when the mode needs a taller box so that `add_icon`'s 1.4.0 auto-sizing still rescues an authored four-line caption; and `_planned_layers` (6.5), which is what makes "a layer that would be empty is not created" decidable before the first edge is added.
15. **`drg_route_table` is a gate but not a layer-gated one** (2.3). `GATE_LAYERS` holds only `badges_routes` and `badges_security`. A badge is drawn on a corner that already exists, so emitting it because the "routes" layer is on costs no space; the DRG route-table strip is laid out inside the DRG cluster and changes its height, so emitting it for the layer would move pixels and break V1.
16. **`_edge_label` gates on `edge_labels` alone, and the minimal mode sets that gate** (2.7, 5.4). Spec 6.3 and 6.4 both drop connector labels, by different routes; implementing them as two independent tests inside the layout helper gives two places that can disagree and makes an explicit `edge_labels` model key unreachable. `resolve_view` instead sets `edge_labels` False as a DEFAULT when `label_mode` was explicitly chosen as `minimal`, so a level that merely happens to select `minimal` (executive, application) still follows its own table row.
17. **`discovery` is keyword-only; `inferred` keeps the fifth positional slot** (11.8). The reviewer-proposed `new_edge(source, target, label, kind, *, discovery, inferred)` turns `query_tenancy._route_edges`' existing `new_edge(src, id2, label, "control", False)` into a `TypeError` in the middle of Task 11, which ruling 1 forbids. Making only the new parameter keyword-only achieves the same guarantee - the fifth positional can never come to mean `discovery` - with no call site to change.
18. **`prune_dangling` takes a `known=` endpoint whitelist** (4.7, R-C). The recipe's registry resolves endpoint forms the model-address walk never yields: `groups[]` box keys, NSG badge addresses, `services:<vcn>`, plain container names. Judging those as dangling would delete legitimate edges from every diagram, filtered or not. `filter_model` therefore passes the pre-filter address set and an unrecognised form is left alone; `select_vcn` passes nothing and keeps the strict 1.4.0 behaviour.
19. **The layers are created before the model edges and applied after `fit_page`** (6.6). Spec 6.2 step 3 says an edge takes its layer as `parent` at `add_edge` time and a badge is moved with `reparent`; those two happen at different points in the phase order, so the creation is split from the application. `_apply_layers` runs after `fit_page` exactly as the spec says, and cannot change the page size because `reparent` preserves absolute position and `content_bbox` is position-based.
20. **`LABEL_H_DETAILED` is 88 px, not the spec's 76** (1.3). The spec sized the box from the field count (five lines, 68.75 px). The box is rendered from the WRAPPED line count, and `Compartment: app-prod` alone wraps at `LABEL_W = 105`, so the four-field detailed preset is six lines. 76 px would leave every detailed caption clipped, silently: rule 5 is a warning and is only fatal under `--strict`. 88 px is `6 * LABEL_LINE_H + 4`, which is `add_icon`'s own auto-grow height for six lines and clears rule 5's `6 * 11 * 1.25` escape. Cost of the change: 12 px of caption box in the one mode that asks for it.
21. **Three reader- and geometry-level corrections the spec did not anticipate** (2.7, 1.21, 4.7): filter expressions are read by `_as_exprs`, which never splits on `,`, because the comma is 6.5's OR *inside* one expression and `_as_tuple` would destroy the grammar; the legend's `note` row is self-sizing, because the filter report of 6.5 is four expressions and a fixed 22 px row would print through the rows below it and the legend border; and a VCN emptied by a filter is dropped even though it still has gateways, because structure is never predicated and counting it would make `keep_empty` unobservable.

### 3. Placeholder scan

No step says "TBD", "similar to Task N", "add appropriate handling" or "write tests for the above".
Every code step quotes the exact text to find and the exact text to write; every test step carries
the full test body; every run step gives the command and the expected output; every commit step
gives the command. Five steps deliberately leave a value only the machine can supply, and each
names how to get it and where it goes:

- **15.8 / 16.8** - `${FORBIDDEN:?...}`, which is an environment variable **on purpose**: the
  pattern must never be written into the repository.
- **14.4 / 14.8** - the reference diff and the screenshot diff, which the step says to read and what
  to expect in them.
- **16.6** - the visual PNG check, whose acceptance criteria the step lists one per spec rule.
- **16.9** - the conditional fix commit, which exists only if a gate found something and whose
  message is the one-line description of what it found.

Two things that were placeholders in an earlier draft are now resolved in the plan itself: the OCI
Search tag-predicate finding (12.1 names the three source pages and states the finding; 12.9 quotes
the docstring verbatim, and `DocstringTests` plus a grep in 16.8 prove it was not left unfilled),
and the READMEs' test count (neither README quotes one today, so 15.7 adds none).

Three doc steps **describe** content instead of quoting it, which is accepted for prose:
**15.4** (`SKILL.md`'s three new tables and the updated phase list), **15.5**
(`oracle-styles.md`'s tenancy and view-layer sections) and **15.7** (the two READMEs and the repo
`CLAUDE.md`). Each names the file, the section, the facts to state and, where a convention exists,
the convention to follow; none of them is asserted on by a test except through the version grep.

`grep -nE "TBD|TODO|FIXME|as appropriate|and so on|similar to Task"` over this plan returns only
the first line of this subsection and this line, both of which quote the words in order to rule
them out. The `...` occurrences are inside
quoted CLI output (`Ran N tests ... OK`, `OK: no container overlaps or layout errors (...)`) and
inside quoted Python argument lists the surrounding step spells out in full.

### 4. Type consistency

- **`view`** is produced only by `oci_view.resolve_view` (2.7) and is a `dict` with exactly these
  keys and types: `purpose: Optional[str]`, `detail: str`, `label_mode: str`,
  `label_fields: Tuple[str, ...]`, `label_tag_keys: Tuple[str, ...]`, `layers_mode: str`,
  `layers: Tuple[str, ...]`, `hidden_layers: Tuple[str, ...]`, `base_layer_name: str`,
  `filter: {"include": Tuple[str, ...], "exclude": Tuple[str, ...], "keep_empty": bool}`,
  `mode: str`, `discovery: Optional[Tuple[str, ...]]`, `annotate_discovery: bool`,
  `global_services: str`, `show_edges: bool`, `show_compartments: bool`, `subnet_label: str`,
  `legend: Optional[bool]`, `line_budget: int`, `notes: List[str]`, and one `bool` per
  `GATE_KEYS` entry. Every layout helper reads it with `.get` and falls back to `_DEFAULT_VIEW`
  (5.4), so a 1.4.0 call site and a direct unit-test call both keep working. `layout_info["view"]`
  is the same object.
- **`ctx`** (`_view_ctx`) keeps its v1.4.0 keys - `locations`, `gateway_edge`, `subnet_label`,
  `attachment_style`, `show_compartments`, `internet`, `third_party`, `has_internet` - and gains
  exactly one, `view`. `ctx["subnet_label"]` and `ctx["show_compartments"]` are
  `view["subnet_label"]` and `view["show_compartments"]` from 5.5 on, so the two can never
  diverge and a detail level or a purpose can set either; `oci_topology.subnet_label_mode` and
  `oci_topology.show_compartments` leave the `oci_layout` import list in the same step.
- **`layers`** has one spelling per level: the model / CLI value is `"off" | "auto" | str csv |
  List[str] | bool`; `resolve_view` collapses it to `layers_mode: str` plus
  `layers: Tuple[str, ...]` whose members are always in `VIEW_LAYERS`; `_create_layers` maps those
  to `Dict[str, str]` (name -> cell id, ids `layer-<name>`); `layout_info["layers"]` is
  `{"enabled": List[str], "hidden": List[str], "cells": Dict[str, int]}`. `hidden_layers` is
  always a subset of `layers` (2.7 filters it), so a legend row can never name a layer that does
  not exist. `resolve_view` reads the raw value from the override and the model only, never from a
  preset: a purpose sets the layer SET, never the layer MODE.
- **`discovery`** is `None` (every kind) or a `Tuple[str, ...]` of `DISCOVERY_KINDS` members
  everywhere: the CLI splits on commas, `resolve_view` validates, `filter_model` compares against
  the edge's `str`. `edges[].discovery` is always a `str` with the default `"association"`, and
  `edges[].inferred` is always a `bool` derived from it - `discovery in INFERRED_DISCOVERY`, and
  `INFERRED_DISCOVERY` is the one-element tuple `("heuristic",)` - unless a caller passed one
  positionally (11.8).
- **the filter spec** has one normaliser, `oci_view._filter_spec` -> `parse_filter`. `_filter_spec`
  reads its lists with `_as_exprs`, never `_as_tuple`, so an expression's internal commas survive,
  and returns `{"include": Tuple[str, ...], "exclude": Tuple[str, ...], "keep_empty": bool}` (raw
  expression strings); `parse_filter` returns the same shape with `List[dict]` values, each
  `{"negate": bool, "dim": str, "key": str, "op": str, "values": Tuple[str, ...], "raw": str}`.
  `view["filter"]` holds the first form, `filter_model` calls `parse_filter` itself, and
  `validate_model` (11.10) validates by calling `parse_filter` - one grammar, one error text.
- **the filter report** is a `dict` with the fixed keys `include`, `exclude` (both
  `Tuple[str, ...]`), `items_kept`, `items_dropped`, `edges_dropped`, `containers_dropped`,
  `pruned_items`, `pruned_services` (all `int`), `mode: str` and `warnings: List[str]`. It reaches
  three readers: `layout_info["filter"]` / `["pruned"]` (8.4, a projection of it),
  `_legend_entries` (10.3) and `summarise()` (11.10) via the transient `model["filter_report"]`,
  which 11.11 and 12.7 pop before serialising.
- **`item["tags"]`** is `{"freeform": Dict[str, str], "defined": Dict[str, str]}` with defined-tag
  keys namespaced `<ns>.<key>`, written by `new_item` (11.7) from `Res.tags` (11.4) and by
  `query_tenancy.normalise_entity` (12.4), read by `oci_view._tags_of` (4.7) and
  `_field_texts("tags")` (2.11), validated by `_validate_item` (11.10). It is never inside
  `metadata`, which keeps its scalar-only contract.
- **`metadata`** stays `Dict[str, str | int | float | bool]`. The five new `METADATA_KEYS` and the
  two computed keys (`compartment`, `ports`) are all strings; a `None` value is never written, so a
  missing field is a missing key and the renderer skips it silently.
- **`service_scope`** returns exactly one of `SERVICE_SCOPES` and is the only classifier;
  `is_global` and `is_regional` are both defined in terms of it (3.4), and `_split_services`
  (9.4) is the only place that turns it into three lists.
- **`reg.layer_cells`** is `Dict[str, List[str]]` keyed by a `VIEW_LAYERS` name, written in exactly
  four places (6.4's three badge helpers and 9.5's bucket) and read in exactly two
  (`_planned_layers`, `_apply_layers`). `reg.badge_kinds` keeps its four strings and its single
  reader, `LEGEND_BADGE_ROWS`.
- **Cell ids** added by 1.5.0: `layer-<name>` for the six layers, `global` for the bucket and
  `global-compartments` for its text cell. All go through `_new_id(key)` and therefore `_slug()`,
  none collides with an address, a `groups[].key`, `tenancy` or `compartment-<name>`, and 9.1
  `test_the_key_global_never_collides_with_the_compartment_wrapper` pins the one that could.
- **`layout_info`** stays a `dict`, keeping the 1.4.0 keys `topology`, `warnings` (`List[str]`),
  `drg_style`, `locations`, `canvas`, `compartments` and gaining `view` (the dict above), `layers`,
  `filter`, `pruned` (`{"items": int, "services": int}`), `edges` (`Dict[str, int]`, the discovery
  mix) and `global_services` (`str`).
